DTS Overnight Camp 2012 
The DTS Overnight Camp is a 5 full day camp designed to 
provide a rigorous training program in combination with the 
overnight experience of a soccer camp within a private school 
setting. By challenging players to think and act in specific game 
situation they will improve greatly in a short period of time. 
The camp is led by our DTS Staff, all of which have KNVB and 
UEFA licenses. Training will focus on the Dutch Concept of 
training: Knowing WHAT to do, WHEN to do it and HOW to do it 
at the technical and tactical speed that is required in the modern 
game. In addition to soccer practices, games, scrimmages and 
instruction, the camp will include team building and other 
activities to keep your child interested in the entire weeks 
activities. 
The camp is for girls and boys from 9 to 15 years of age, with 
housing dedicated to each. In addition to our trainers, the staff 
will include female chaperones and on site medical staff.

Hello again - 
I hope the following makes sense as I just had a large glass of wine with dinner! 
The hourly rate is 100 dollars, but i have to be extremely clear here. I work a huge 
amount of each day, but you are only billed for time I am actively 
searching for things for you for a half hour or more or when I am in 
front of you. You will be able to account for all the time, and it does 
not add up to much, typically. It is a way to cover me if clients decide 
to do purchasing on their own after doing creative with me first, and 
also to cover expenses. If you want to shop with me for hours and 
hours, as some clients in the past have, without making any purchases, 
then I am covered. However, i really need to impress upon you that 
this is not a significant amount of billing, typically. 
In the beginning, if we arc doing drawings and a huge number of creative boards, it 
may seem like a lot of hours, but those are key to the process, and ?m 
pretty quick at that too. Once we are up and running, it moves pretty 
fast. 
EFTA_R1_00853441 
EFTA02171439

but here again, I have to be very clear in making sure you understand

discounted price. Also, you have total access to all of your 
paperwork - that is you can see my books anytime and may take your 
receipts with you at the conclusion of the job for reference, so it's all 
very open-book. Any of my vendors will tell you the real price I pay 
for anything. 
I do not mark up electrical work or plumbing, although I can set it up for you and

CHECK-IN 
You may be able to check in online 24 hours before your flight and save time at the airport. 
For domestic travel (United States. Puerto Rico and U.S. Virgin Islands). we recommend you 
present yourself at the airport 2 hours prior to departure. For international travel, we recommend you 
present yourself at the airportat least 3 hours prior to departure. 
Please be sure to bring appropriate documentation for travel. For more information on required 
documentation for international travel, please contact the embassy or consulate of the country to 
which you are traveling.

for Spanish).

number at least 24 hours prior to the departure of any flight segment to make any changes. Advance 
notification is required to avoid forfeiting the value of your reservation. Click here for information on 
travel policies.
